We introduce EgoVerse, a collaborative platform for human data-driven robot learning that unifies data collection, processing, and access under a shared framework, enabling contributions from individual researchers, academic labs, and industry partners. The current release includes 1,362 hours (80k episodes) of human demonstrations spanning 1,965 tasks, 240 scenes, and 2,087 unique demonstrators, with standardized formats, manipulation-relevant annotations, and tooling for downstream learning. Beyond the dataset, we conduct a large-scale study of human-to-robot transfer with experiments replicated across multiple labs, tasks, and robot embodiments under shared protocols. We find that policy performance generally improves with increased human data, but that effective scaling depends on alignment between human data and robot learning objectives. Together, the dataset, platform, and study establish a foundation for reproducible progress in human data-driven robot learning. Videos and additional information can be found at https://egoverse.ai/

</details>


### [110] [Safe Large-Scale Robust Nonlinear MPC in Milliseconds via Reachability-Constrained System Level Synthesis on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07644)
*Jeffrey Fang,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPU-SLS：基于GPU并行化的安全鲁棒非线性模型预测控制框架，针对高维不确定机器人系统和长规划时域，实现实时联合优化名义轨迹、跟踪控制器和闭环可达集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理高维不确定机器人系统（如全身四足机器人61D、人形机器人75D）和长规划时域时，计算效率低下，难以实现实时安全控制。传统CPU求解器无法满足实时性要求，而现有GPU求解器性能仍有提升空间，且学习型可达性方法在高维场景下无法保证100%安全性。

Method: 1. 开发GPU并行化框架，联合优化不等式约束的动态可行名义轨迹、跟踪控制器和扰动下的闭环可达集；2. 提出基于顺序二次规划的GPU加速QP求解器，采用并行关联扫描和自适应缓存的ADMM框架；3. 使用相同GPU QP后端通过系统级合成优化鲁棒跟踪控制器和闭环可达集；4. 支持固定时域和滚动时域下的可达性约束控制。

Result: 1. 性能显著提升：名义轨迹求解时间相比最先进CPU求解器减少97.7%，相比GPU求解器减少71.8%；SLS控制与可达性计算加速237倍；2. 安全性：实现100%经验安全性，优于高维学习型可达性基线；3. 实时性：在GPU上平均20毫秒在线合成鲁棒控制策略；4. 可扩展性：支持2×10^5决策变量和8×10^4约束的大规模问题；5. 验证系统：复杂非线性系统包括全身四足机器人（61D）和人形机器人（75D）。

Conclusion: GPU-SLS框架通过GPU并行化和新颖的QP求解器设计，实现了高维不确定机器人系统的实时安全鲁棒控制，显著提升了计算效率并保证了100%经验安全性，为复杂机器人系统的在线控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We present GPU-SLS, a GPU-parallelized framework for safe, robust nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) that scales to high-dimensional uncertain robotic systems and long planning horizons. Our method jointly optimizes an inequality-constrained, dynamically-feasible nominal trajectory, a tracking controller, and a closed-loop reachable set under disturbance, all in real-time. To efficiently compute nominal trajectories, we develop a sequential quadratic programming procedure with a novel GPU-accelerated quadratic program (QP) solver that uses parallel associative scans and adaptive caching within an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) framework. The same GPU QP backend is used to optimize robust tracking controllers and closed-loop reachable sets via system level synthesis (SLS), enabling reachability-constrained control in both fixed- and receding-horizon settings. We achieve substantial performance gains, reducing nominal trajectory solve times by 97.7% relative to state-of-the-art CPU solvers and 71.8% compared to GPU solvers, while accelerating SLS-based control and reachability by 237x. Despite large problem scales, our method achieves 100% empirical safety, unlike high-dimensional learning-based reachability baselines. We validate our approach on complex nonlinear systems, including whole-body quadrupeds (61D) and humanoids (75D), synthesizing robust control policies online on the GPU in 20 milliseconds on average and scaling to problems with 2 x 10^5 decision variables and 8 x 10^4 constraints. The implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/Jeff300fang/gpu_sls.

</details>


### [111] [Reset-Free Reinforcement Learning for Real-World Agile Driving: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07672)
*Kohei Honda,Hirotaka Hosogaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无重置强化学习在真实世界敏捷驾驶中的应用，发现仿真与真实世界存在显著差距，TD-MPC2在物理平台上表现最佳，而残差学习在真实世界中未能发挥优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究真实世界无重置强化学习在高速敏捷驾驶中的应用挑战。由于复杂的车辆动力学、执行延迟和其他未建模效应，基于学习的方法在轮胎摩擦极限附近的高速驾驶中面临困难，这既阻碍了精确仿真，也影响了学习策略的直接仿真到真实迁移。

Method: 使用模型预测路径积分控制（MPPI）作为重置策略和残差学习的基础策略。系统比较了三种代表性RL算法（PPO、SAC、TD-MPC2）在仿真和真实世界实验中的表现，包括使用和不使用残差学习的情况。在物理1/10比例车辆上进行连续学习，无需人工重置。

Result: 仿真与真实世界存在明显差距：SAC+残差学习在仿真中获得最高回报，但只有TD-MPC2在物理平台上持续优于MPPI基线。残差学习在仿真中明显有益，但未能将其优势迁移到真实世界，甚至可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 真实世界中的无重置强化学习提出了仿真中不存在的独特挑战，需要进一步开发专门针对野外训练的算法。TD-MPC2在物理平台上表现最佳，而残差学习在真实世界中的效果有限。

Abstract: This paper presents an empirical study of reset-free reinforcement learning (RL) for real-world agile driving, in which a physical 1/10-scale vehicle learns continuously on a slippery indoor track without manual resets. High-speed driving near the limits of tire friction is particularly challenging for learning-based methods because complex vehicle dynamics, actuation delays, and other unmodeled effects hinder both accurate simulation and direct sim-to-real transfer of learned policies. To enable autonomous training on a physical platform, we employ Model Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI) as both the reset policy and the base policy for residual learning, and systematically compare three representative RL algorithms, i.e., PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2, with and without residual learning in simulation and real-world experiments. Our results reveal a clear gap between simulation and real-world: SAC with residual learning achieves the highest returns in simulation, yet only TD-MPC2 consistently outperforms the MPPI baseline on the physical platform. Moreover, residual learning, while clearly beneficial in simulation, fails to transfer its advantage to the real world and can even degrade performance. These findings reveal that reset-free RL in the real world poses unique challenges absent from simulation, calling for further algorithmic development tailored to training in the wild.

</details>


### [112] [Bird-Inspired Spatial Flapping Wing Mechanism via Coupled Linkages with Single Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07677)
*Daniel Huczala,Sun-Pill Jung,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于耦合空间四杆机构的鸟翼仿生扑翼机制，采用单电机驱动，通过被动状态切换实现协调的扫掠-折叠翼运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间单环机构（如Bennett连杆）具有单自由度驱动和非平凡空间轨迹的优点，适用于轻量化仿生机器人设计。然而，其几何任务综合的数学复杂性导致工程应用中往往避免使用。本文旨在简化Bennett连杆的构建方法，并利用被动切换机制实现单驱动下的协调扑翼运动。

Method: 提出了一种简化的运动学方法，从包含所需表面积的四边形构建Bennett连杆。采用两个耦合的空间四杆机构：一个主动驱动产生空间扫掠行程，另一个串联耦合的连杆保持非驱动状态，在行程周期中被动地在伸展和折叠翼配置之间切换。

Result: 通过3D打印原型组装和测试，验证了该机制能够实现预期的空间行程和被动折叠行为。该架构以单一驱动输入实现了协调的扫掠-折叠翼运动，降低了重量和控制复杂性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的简化Bennett连杆构建方法和被动状态切换机制，成功实现了单驱动下的仿生扑翼运动，为轻量化生物启发机器人设计提供了一种有效的解决方案，克服了传统空间连杆机构几何综合的复杂性障碍。

Abstract: Spatial single-loop mechanisms such as Bennett linkages offer a unique combination of one-degree-of-freedom actuation and nontrivial spatial trajectories, making them attractive for lightweight bio-inspired robotic design. However, although they appear simple and elegant, the geometric task-based synthesis is rather complicated and often avoided in engineering tasks due to the mathematical complexity involved. This paper presents a bird-inspired flapping-wing mechanism built from two coupled spatial four-bars, driven by a single motor. One linkage is actuated to generate the desired spatial sweeping stroke, while the serially coupled linkage remains unactuated and passively switches between extended and folded wing configurations over the stroke cycle. We introduce a simplified kinematic methodology for constructing Bennett linkages from quadrilaterals that contain a desired surface area and further leverage mechanically induced passive state switching. This architecture realizes a coordinated sweep-and-fold wing motion with a single actuation input, reducing weight and control complexity. A 3D-printed prototype is assembled and tested, demonstrating the intended spatial stroke and passive folding behavior.

</details>


### [113] [Vision-Language Navigation for Aerial Robots: Towards the Era of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07705)
*Xingyu Xia,Lekai Zhou,Yujie Tang,Xiaozhou Zhu,Hai Zhu,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对空中视觉语言导航（Aerial VLN）领域进行了系统性综述，重点关注大型语言模型和视觉语言模型的集成，提出了方法分类、评估体系分析、关键挑战和七个开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机技术和多模态人工智能的发展，空中视觉语言导航成为重要研究方向。本文旨在系统梳理该领域的研究现状，特别是LLM/VLM集成带来的新机遇，分析现有方法的优缺点，识别评估基础设施的不足，并为未来研究提供明确方向。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法：1）正式定义Aerial VLN问题，区分单指令和对话式两种交互范式；2）将现有方法归纳为五类架构：序列到序列与注意力方法、端到端LLM/VLM方法、分层方法、多智能体方法、对话式导航方法；3）分析评估基础设施（数据集、仿真平台、指标）的局限性；4）在共享基准上比较不同方法；5）识别关键架构权衡。

Result: 建立了Aerial VLN方法的分类体系，揭示了现有评估基础设施在规模、环境多样性、真实世界接地和指标覆盖方面的不足。通过跨方法比较分析了离散vs连续动作、端到端vs分层设计、仿真到现实差距等关键权衡。识别了当前方法在长时程指令接地、视点鲁棒性等方面的局限性。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地综述了Aerial VLN领域，提出了七个具体开放问题：长时程指令接地、视点鲁棒性、可扩展空间表示、连续6自由度动作执行、机载部署、基准标准化、多无人机集群导航。这些方向基于综述中的证据，为未来研究提供了明确路线图。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (Aerial VLN) aims to enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to interpret natural language instructions and autonomously navigate complex three-dimensional environments by grounding language in visual perception. This survey provides a critical and analytical review of the Aerial VLN field, with particular attention to the recent integration of large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs). We first formally introduce the Aerial VLN problem and define two interaction paradigms: single-instruction and dialog-based, as foundational axes. We then organize the body of Aerial VLN methods into a taxonomy of five architectural categories: sequence-to-sequence and attention-based methods, end-to-end LLM/VLM methods, hierarchical methods, multi-agent methods, and dialog-based navigation methods. For each category, we systematically analyze design rationales, technical trade-offs, and reported performance. We critically assess the evaluation infrastructure for Aerial VLN, including datasets, simulation platforms, and metrics, and identify their gaps in scale, environmental diversity, real-world grounding, and metric coverage. We consolidate cross-method comparisons on shared benchmarks and analyze key architectural trade-offs, including discrete versus continuous actions, end-to-end versus hierarchical designs, and the simulation-to-reality gap. Finally, we synthesize seven concrete open problems: long-horizon instruction grounding, viewpoint robustness, scalable spatial representation, continuous 6-DoF action execution, onboard deployment, benchmark standardization, and multi-UAV swarm navigation, with specific research directions grounded in the evidence presented throughout the survey.

</details>


### [114] [RoboAgent: Chaining Basic Capabilities for Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07774)
*Peiran Xu,Jiaqi Zheng,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAgent：一种基于能力驱动的具身任务规划框架，通过调度器主动调用不同子能力，将复杂规划分解为VLM能更好处理的基本视觉语言问题，实现更透明可控的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在多模态理解和推理方面取得显著进展，但在涉及多轮交互、长时程推理和扩展上下文分析的具身规划任务中表现仍然有限。需要一种能有效分解复杂规划、实现透明可控推理的框架。

Method: 提出RoboAgent能力驱动规划管道：1) 调度器主动调用不同子能力，每个能力维护自身上下文；2) 子能力根据调度器查询产生中间推理结果或与环境交互；3) 所有组件均用单一VLM实现，无需外部工具；4) 采用三阶段训练范式：行为克隆、DAgger训练和强化学习，利用环境模拟器内部信息构建高质量监督，并引入增强和合成数据提升多样性场景性能。

Result: 在广泛使用的具身任务规划基准测试中进行了大量实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。代码将在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: RoboAgent框架通过能力驱动的方法将复杂具身规划分解为VLM能更好处理的基本问题，实现了更透明可控的推理过程，为解决VLM在具身任务规划中的局限性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on embodied task planning, where an agent acquires visual observations from the environment and executes atomic actions to accomplish a given task. Although recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results in multimodal understanding and reasoning, their performance remains limited when applied to embodied planning that involves multi-turn interaction, long-horizon reasoning, and extended context analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose RoboAgent, a capability-driven planning pipeline in which the model actively invokes different sub-capabilities. Each capability maintains its own context, and produces intermediate reasoning results or interacts with the environment according to the query given by a scheduler. This framework decomposes complex planning into a sequence of basic vision-language problems that VLMs can better address, enabling a more transparent and controllable reasoning process. The scheduler and all capabilities are implemented with a single VLM, without relying on external tools. To train this VLM, we adopt a multi-stage paradigm that consists of: (1) behavior cloning with expert plans, (2) DAgger training using trajectories collected by the model, and (3) reinforcement learning guided by an expert policy. Across these stages, we exploit the internal information of the environment simulator to construct high-quality supervision for each capability, and we further introduce augmented and synthetic data to enhance the model's performance in more diverse scenarios. Extensive experiments on widely used embodied task planning benchmarks validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. Our codes will be available at https://github.com/woyut/RoboAgent_CVPR26.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Without Losing Identity: Capability Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07799)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出能力中心演化范式，将具身智能体的认知身份与能力演化分离，通过模块化、版本化的具身能力模块实现持续改进，在保持身份稳定性的同时提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过修改智能体本身（提示工程、策略更新、结构重设计）来提升性能，导致长期系统中不稳定和身份丢失。需要一种既能持续改进又能保持智能体认知身份持久性的方法。

Method: 引入具身能力模块概念，作为模块化、版本化的具身功能单元。提出统一框架，将能力演化与智能体身份解耦。能力通过任务执行、经验收集、模型精炼和模块更新的闭环过程演化，所有执行由运行时层管理以确保安全和策略约束。

Result: 在模拟具身任务中，能力演化在20次迭代后将任务成功率从32.4%提升至91.3%，优于智能体修改基线和现有技能学习方法（SPiRL、SkiMo），同时保持零策略漂移和零安全违规。

Conclusion: 将智能体身份与能力演化分离为长期具身智能提供了可扩展且安全的基础，使智能体在保持认知身份的同时实现持续性能改进。

Abstract: Embodied agents are expected to operate persistently in dynamic physical environments, continuously acquiring new capabilities over time. Existing approaches to improving agent performance often rely on modifying the agent itself -- through prompt engineering, policy updates, or structural redesign -- leading to instability and loss of identity in long-lived systems. In this work, we propose a capability-centric evolution paradigm for embodied agents. We argue that a robot should maintain a persistent agent as its cognitive identity, while enabling continuous improvement through the evolution of its capabilities. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Embodied Capability Modules (ECMs), which represent modular, versioned units of embodied functionality that can be learned, refined, and composed over time. We present a unified framework in which capability evolution is decoupled from agent identity. Capabilities evolve through a closed-loop process involving task execution, experience collection, model refinement, and module updating, while all executions are governed by a runtime layer that enforces safety and policy constraints. We demonstrate through simulated embodied tasks that capability evolution improves task success rates from 32.4% to 91.3% over 20 iterations, outperforming both agent-modification baselines and established skill-learning methods (SPiRL, SkiMo), while preserving zero policy drift and zero safety violations. Our results suggest that separating agent identity from capability evolution provides a scalable and safe foundation for long-term embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [116] [Harnessing Embodied Agents: Runtime Governance for Policy-Constrained Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07833)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于具身智能体的运行时治理框架，将认知与执行监督分离，通过外部化治理层实现策略检查、能力准入、执行监控等功能，显著提升动作可控性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身智能体从被动推理系统演变为与工具、机器人和物理环境交互的主动执行器，一旦获得执行权限，核心挑战在于如何在运行时保持动作的可治理性。现有方法将安全和恢复逻辑嵌入智能体循环内部，导致执行控制难以标准化、审计和适应。

Method: 提出策略约束执行框架，将智能体认知与执行监督分离。治理被外部化为专门的运行时层，执行策略检查、能力准入、执行监控、回滚处理和人工覆盖。形式化定义了具身智能体、具身能力模块和运行时治理层之间的控制边界。

Result: 通过1000次随机模拟试验在三个治理维度上进行验证：96.2%的未授权动作被拦截；运行时漂移下的不安全延续从100%降至22.2%；91.4%的恢复成功率且完全符合策略要求。所有结果均显著优于基线（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 通过将运行时治理重构为一类系统问题，将策略约束执行定位为具身智能体系统的关键设计原则。该框架不仅需要更强的智能体，更需要更强的运行时治理。

Abstract: Embodied agents are evolving from passive reasoning systems into active executors that interact with tools, robots, and physical environments. Once granted execution authority, the central challenge becomes how to keep actions governable at runtime. Existing approaches embed safety and recovery logic inside the agent loop, making execution control difficult to standardize, audit, and adapt.
  This paper argues that embodied intelligence requires not only stronger agents, but stronger runtime governance. We propose a framework for policy-constrained execution that separates agent cognition from execution oversight. Governance is externalized into a dedicated runtime layer performing policy checking, capability admission, execution monitoring, rollback handling, and human override.
  We formalize the control boundary among the embodied agent, Embodied Capability Modules (ECMs), and runtime governance layer, and validate through 1000 randomized simulation trials across three governance dimensions. Results show 96.2% interception of unauthorized actions, reduction of unsafe continuation from 100% to 22.2% under runtime drift, and 91.4% recovery success with full policy compliance, substantially outperforming all baselines (p<0.001). By reframing runtime governance as a first-class systems problem, this paper positions policy-constrained execution as a key design principle for embodied agent systems.

</details>


### [117] [The Sustainability Gap in Robotics: A Large-Scale Survey of Sustainability Awareness in 50,000 Research Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07921)
*Antun Skuric,Leandro Von Werra,Thomas Wolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对arXiv cs.RO类别近5万篇论文的大规模调查显示，机器人学研究中可持续性沟通和动机存在显著差距：尽管许多研究可映射到联合国可持续发展目标相关领域，但明确提及可持续性影响的比例低于2%，直接引用SDG的不足0.1%，可持续性动机论文占比低于5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估机器人学研究领域对可持续性沟通和动机的现状，量化该领域在应对社会、生态和可持续性挑战方面的意识水平和研究意图，揭示机器人学技术潜力与实际研究动机之间的差距。

Method: 对arXiv cs.RO类别2015年至2026年初发表的近50,000篇开放获取论文进行大规模文本分析，量化论文中提及社会、生态和可持续性影响的频率，分析其与联合国可持续发展目标（SDGs）的对应关系。

Result: 研究发现机器人学领域存在明显的意识与动机差距：可持续性相关影响提及率通常低于2%，明确SDG引用率低于0.1%，可持续性动机论文比例低于5%。尽管大量机器人学研究可映射到SDG相关领域，但可持续性尚未成为研究框架的标准组成部分。

Conclusion: 机器人学领域虽快速发展，但可持续性尚未成为研究框架的核心要素。研究建议研究人员、会议和机构采取具体行动，弥合意识与动机差距，推动更负责任和有意向的创新。

Abstract: We present a large-scale survey of sustainability communication and motivation in robotics research. Our analysis covers nearly 50,000 open-access papers from arXiv's cs.RO category published between 2015 and early 2026. In this study, we quantify how often papers mention social, ecological, and sustainability impacts, and we analyse their alignment with the UN Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).
  The results reveal a persistent gap between the field's potential and its stated intent. While a large fraction of robotics papers can be mapped to SDG-relevant domains, explicit sustainability motivation remains remarkably low. Specifically, mentions of sustainability-related impacts are typically below 2%, explicit SDG references stay below 0.1%, and the proportion of sustainability-motivated papers remains below 5%. These trends suggest that while the field of robotics is advancing rapidly, sustainability is not yet a standard part of research framing.
  We conclude by proposing concrete actions for researchers, conferences, and institutions to close these awareness and motivation gaps, supporting a shift toward more intentional and responsible innovation.

</details>


### [118] [RAGE-XY: RADAR-Aided Longitudinal and Lateral Forces Estimation For Autonomous Race Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07939)
*Davide Malvezzi,Nicola Musiu,Eugenio Mascaro,Francesco Iacovacci,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAGE-XY是RAGE框架的扩展版本，通过在线RADAR校准模块和三轮车模型改进车辆动力学估计精度，能够实时估计车辆速度、轮胎滑移角和受力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始RAGE框架存在传感器未对准时的横向速度估计精度不足问题，且仅使用单轨模型限制了纵向力估计能力。需要改进框架以更准确估计车辆横向和纵向动力学。

Method: 1) 引入在线RADAR校准模块处理传感器未对准问题；2) 将基础车辆模型从单轨近似扩展到三轮车模型，支持后轮纵向轮胎力估计；3) 仅使用标准车载传感器（IMU和RADAR）。

Result: 在高保真仿真和EAV-24自动驾驶赛车真实实验中验证，RAGE-XY在估计车辆横向和纵向动力学方面表现出更高的精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RAGE-XY通过在线传感器校准和改进的车辆模型，显著提升了车辆动力学估计性能，为自动驾驶系统提供了更可靠的实时状态估计能力。

Abstract: In this work, we present RAGE-XY, an extended version of RAGE, a real-time estimation framework that simultaneously infers vehicle velocity, tire slip angles, and the forces acting on the vehicle using only standard onboard sensors such as IMUs and RADARs. Compared to the original formulation, the proposed method incorporates an online RADAR calibration module, improving the accuracy of lateral velocity estimation in the presence of sensor misalignment. Furthermore, we extend the underlying vehicle model from a single-track approximation to a tricycle model, enabling the estimation of rear longitudinal tire forces in addition to lateral dynamics. We validate the proposed approach through both high-fidelity simulations and real-world experiments conducted on the EAV-24 autonomous race car, demonstrating improved accuracy and robustness in estimating both lateral and longitudinal vehicle dynamics.

</details>


### [119] [On-Policy Distillation of Language Models for Autonomous Vehicle Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07944)
*Amirhossein Afsharrad,Amirhesam Abedsoltan,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Sanjay Lall*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何将大型语言模型在自动驾驶运动规划中的知识迁移到更小的可部署学生模型中，提出了基于策略的广义知识蒸馏方法，在模型尺寸减小5倍的情况下仍能接近教师模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动驾驶运动规划中展现出潜力，但部署到资源受限的车载系统存在挑战。需要将大型教师LLM的运动规划知识有效迁移到更小、更可部署的学生模型中。

Method: 基于GPT-Driver框架，将驾驶场景表示为语言提示，通过思维链推理生成轨迹点。研究了两种学生训练范式：1) 基于策略的广义知识蒸馏，使用教师模型的密集令牌级反馈训练学生自身生成的输出；2) 密集反馈强化学习基线，使用教师模型的log概率作为策略梯度框架中的每令牌奖励信号。

Result: 在nuScenes基准测试中，广义知识蒸馏显著优于强化学习基线，在模型尺寸减小5倍的情况下仍能接近教师级性能。

Conclusion: 基于策略的蒸馏作为一种原则性且有效的方法，对于在自动驾驶系统中部署基于LLM的规划器具有实际价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong potential for autonomous vehicle motion planning by reformulating trajectory prediction as a language generation problem. However, deploying capable LLMs in resource-constrained onboard systems remains a fundamental challenge. In this paper, we study how to effectively transfer motion planning knowledge from a large teacher LLM to a smaller, more deployable student model. We build on the GPT-Driver framework, which represents driving scenes as language prompts and generates waypoint trajectories with chain-of-thought reasoning, and investigate two student training paradigms: (i) on-policy generalized knowledge distillation (GKD), which trains the student on its own self-generated outputs using dense token-level feedback from the teacher, and (ii) a dense-feedback reinforcement learning (RL) baseline that uses the teacher's log-probabilities as per-token reward signals in a policy gradient framework. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that GKD substantially outperforms the RL baseline and closely approaches teacher-level performance despite a 5$\times$ reduction in model size. These results highlight the practical value of on-policy distillation as a principled and effective approach to deploying LLM-based planners in autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [120] [Incremental Residual Reinforcement Learning Toward Real-World Learning for Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07945)
*Haruto Nagahisa,Kohei Matsumoto,Yuki Tomita,Yuki Hyodo,Ryo Kurazume*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出增量残差强化学习(IRRL)，结合增量学习和残差强化学习，解决移动机器人社交导航中计算资源受限和在线学习效率问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人需求增长，社交导航成为关键任务。由于不同地区行人动态和社会规范差异大，仿真难以覆盖所有真实场景。真实世界RL直接在物理环境中学习是解决方案，但面临边缘设备计算资源受限和学习效率低的挑战

Method: 提出增量残差强化学习(IRRL)，集成增量学习（轻量级过程，无需回放缓冲区或批量更新）和残差强化学习（仅训练相对于基础策略的残差来提高学习效率）

Result: 仿真实验表明，尽管没有回放缓冲区，IRRL性能与基于回放缓冲区的传统方法相当，并优于现有增量学习方法。真实世界实验证实IRRL能使机器人通过真实世界学习有效适应未见环境

Conclusion: IRRL为移动机器人社交导航提供了一种有效的真实世界RL方法，解决了计算资源受限和学习效率问题，使机器人能够适应不同地区的行人动态和社会规范

Abstract: As the demand for mobile robots continues to increase, social navigation has emerged as a critical task, driving active research into deep reinforcement learning (RL) approaches. However, because pedestrian dynamics and social conventions vary widely across different regions, simulations cannot easily encompass all possible real-world scenarios. Real-world RL, in which agents learn while operating directly in physical environments, presents a promising solution to this issue. Nevertheless, this approach faces significant challenges, particularly regarding constrained computational resources on edge devices and learning efficiency. In this study, we propose incremental residual RL (IRRL). This method integrates incremental learning, which is a lightweight process that operates without a replay buffer or batch updates, with residual RL, which enhances learning efficiency by training only on the residuals relative to a base policy. Through the simulation experiments, we demonstrated that, despite lacking a replay buffer, IRRL achieved performance comparable to those of conventional replay buffer-based methods and outperformed existing incremental learning approaches. Furthermore, the real-world experiments confirmed that IRRL can enable robots to effectively adapt to previously unseen environments through the real-world learning.

</details>


### [121] [HEX: Humanoid-Aligned Experts for Cross-Embodiment Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07993)
*Shuanghao Bai,Meng Li,Xinyuan Lv,Jiawei Wang,Xinhua Wang,Fei Liao,Chengkai Hou,Langzhe Gu,Wanqi Zhou,Kun Wu,Ziluo Ding,Zhiyuan Xu,Lei Sun,Shanghang Zhang,Zhengping Che,Jian Tang,Badong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HEX是一个面向双足人形机器人的状态中心化协调操作框架，通过统一状态表示、混合专家本体感知预测器和轻量历史标记等技术，实现了高效的全身体协调控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过协调的全身控制实现复杂操作，而大多数视觉-语言-动作模型将机器人身体部位视为独立部分，导致高自由度人形机器人控制困难且不稳定。需要一种能够实现协调全身操作的方法。

Method: 1. 引入人形对齐的通用状态表示，支持跨异构实现的规模化学习；2. 采用混合专家统一本体感知预测器，从大规模多实现轨迹数据中建模全身协调和时序运动动态；3. 使用轻量历史标记总结过去观察，避免推理时重复编码历史图像；4. 采用残差门控融合机制和流匹配动作头，自适应整合视觉-语言线索与本体感知动态以生成动作。

Result: 在真实世界人形机器人操作任务上的实验表明，HEX在任务成功率和泛化能力方面达到最先进性能，特别是在快速反应和长时域场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: HEX框架通过状态中心化方法、统一表示和高效时序建模，成功实现了双足人形机器人的协调全身操作，解决了现有VLA模型在高自由度人形控制中的稳定性和协调性问题。

Abstract: Humans achieve complex manipulation through coordinated whole-body control, whereas most Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models treat robot body parts largely independently, making high-DoF humanoid control challenging and often unstable. We present HEX, a state-centric framework for coordinated manipulation on full-sized bipedal humanoid robots. HEX introduces a humanoid-aligned universal state representation for scalable learning across heterogeneous embodiments, and incorporates a Mixture-of-Experts Unified Proprioceptive Predictor to model whole-body coordination and temporal motion dynamics from large-scale multi-embodiment trajectory data. To efficiently capture temporal visual context, HEX uses lightweight history tokens to summarize past observations, avoiding repeated encoding of historical images during inference. It further employs a residual-gated fusion mechanism with a flow-matching action head to adaptively integrate visual-language cues with proprioceptive dynamics for action generation. Experiments on real-world humanoid manipulation tasks show that HEX achieves state-of-the-art performance in task success rate and generalization, particularly in fast-reaction and long-horizon scenarios.

</details>


### [122] [AgiPIX: Bridging Simulation and Reality in Indoor Aerial Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08009)
*Sasanka Kuruppu Arachchige,Juan Jose Garcia,Changda Tian,Lauri Suomela,Panos Trahanias,Adriana Tapus,Joni-Kristian Kämäräinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Agipix是一个开源软硬件平台，专为室内自主飞行和关键资产检查设计，具有紧凑的主动感知系统、容器化ROS 2自主堆栈和逼真数字孪生，支持仿真与真实环境间的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内自主飞行进行关键资产检查在感知、规划、控制和学习方面面临根本性挑战，目前缺乏一个紧凑、主动感知、开源且能在仿真和真实操作中复现的平台。

Method: 开发了Agipix平台，包含：1) 紧凑的硬件同步主动感知平台，配备板载GPU加速计算；2) 容器化ROS 2模块化自主堆栈；3) 硬件平台的逼真数字孪生和可靠UI。通过容器化实现仿真与真实飞行间的零样本迁移。

Result: 在工业室内环境中展示了使用板载传感的轨迹跟踪和探索性能。所有硬件设计、仿真资产和容器化软件均已开源发布并附带文档。

Conclusion: Agipix填补了室内自主飞行平台在紧凑性、主动感知和仿真-真实可复现性方面的空白，为快速迭代和零样本迁移提供了完整解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous indoor flight for critical asset inspection presents fundamental challenges in perception, planning, control, and learning. Despite rapid progress, there is still a lack of a compact, active-sensing, open-source platform that is reproducible across simulation and real-world operation. To address this gap, we present Agipix, a co-designed open hardware and software platform for indoor aerial autonomy and critical asset inspection. Agipix features a compact, hardware-synchronized active-sensing platform with onboard GPU-accelerated compute that is capable of agile flight; a containerized ROS~2-based modular autonomy stack; and a photorealistic digital twin of the hardware platform together with a reliable UI. These elements enable rapid iteration via zero-shot transfer of containerized autonomy components between simulation and real flights. We demonstrate trajectory tracking and exploration performance using onboard sensing in industrial indoor environments. All hardware designs, simulation assets, and containerized software are released openly together with documentation.

</details>


### [123] [Open-Ended Instruction Realization with LLM-Enabled Multi-Planner Scheduling in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08031)
*Jiawei Liu,Xun Gong,Fen Fang,Muli Yang,Bohao Qu,Yunfeng Hu,Hong Chen,Xulei Yang,Qing Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型的指令实现框架，通过调度多个MPC运动规划器将乘客自然语言指令转换为控制信号，实现可解释、可追溯的自动驾驶人机交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机交互研究忽视自动驾驶中乘客的操控需求，自然语言作为直观接口面临挑战：如何将开放式指令转换为控制信号而不牺牲可解释性和可追溯性

Method: 提出指令实现框架：1) 使用LLM解释指令；2) 生成可执行脚本调度多个基于MPC的运动规划器；3) 将规划轨迹转换为控制信号。采用调度中心设计，在不同时间尺度上解耦语义推理与车辆控制

Result: 框架显著提高任务完成率，降低LLM查询成本，安全性和合规性与专用AD方法相当，对LLM推理延迟具有较大容忍度。引入闭环设置下的开放式指令实现基准

Conclusion: 该框架建立了从高级指令到低级动作的透明、可追溯决策链，解决了自动驾驶中自然语言指令实现的关键挑战，为可解释人机交互提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Most Human-Machine Interaction (HMI) research overlooks the maneuvering needs of passengers in autonomous driving (AD). Natural language offers an intuitive interface, yet translating passenger open-ended instructions into control signals, without sacrificing interpretability and traceability, remains a challenge. This study proposes an instruction-realization framework that leverages a large language model (LLM) to interpret instructions, generates executable scripts that schedule multiple model predictive control (MPC)-based motion planners based on real-time feedback, and converts planned trajectories into control signals. This scheduling-centric design decouples semantic reasoning from vehicle control at different timescales, establishing a transparent, traceable decision-making chain from high-level instructions to low-level actions. Due to the absence of high-fidelity evaluation tools, this study introduces a benchmark for open-ended instruction realization in a closed-loop setting. Comprehensive experiments reveal that the framework significantly improves task-completion rates over instruction-realization baselines, reduces LLM query costs, achieves safety and compliance on par with specialized AD approaches, and exhibits considerable tolerance to LLM inference latency. For more qualitative illustrations and a clearer understanding.

</details>


### [124] [Governed Capability Evolution for Embodied Agents: Safe Upgrade, Compatibility Checking, and Runtime Rollback for Embodied Capability Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08059)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种面向具身智能体的生命周期感知升级框架，通过四类兼容性检查和分阶段运行时管道，在能力模块演进时实现安全部署，避免破坏策略约束、执行假设和恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需要持续更新其可执行能力而非重写整个系统，但现有研究分别关注模块化能力打包、能力演进和运行时治理，缺乏对能力模块演进后如何安全部署的系统性解决方案。关键问题是：当具身能力模块演进为新版本时，如何在不破坏策略约束、执行假设或恢复保证的情况下安全部署。

Method: 提出生命周期感知升级框架，将新能力版本视为受治理的部署候选而非立即可执行替换。引入四类升级兼容性检查：接口、策略、行为和恢复检查，并组织为分阶段运行时管道，包括候选验证、沙箱评估、影子部署、门控激活、在线监控和回滚。

Result: 在6轮能力升级、15个随机种子的评估中：朴素升级实现72.9%任务成功率，但最终轮次不安全激活率达60%；受治理升级保持可比较的成功率（67.4%）并在所有轮次维持零不安全激活（Wilcoxon p=0.003）。影子部署揭示40%的回归在仅沙箱评估中不可见，回滚在79.8%的激活后漂移场景中成功。

Conclusion: 将受治理能力演进确立为具身智能体的首要系统问题，提出的生命周期感知升级框架通过系统化兼容性检查和分阶段部署管道，在保持任务成功率的同时显著降低安全风险，为具身智能体的安全持续演进提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents are increasingly expected to improve over time by updating their executable capabilities rather than rewriting the agent itself. Prior work has separately studied modular capability packaging, capability evolution, and runtime governance. However, a key systems problem remains underexplored: once an embodied capability module evolves into a new version, how can the hosting system deploy it safely without breaking policy constraints, execution assumptions, or recovery guarantees?
  We formulate governed capability evolution as a first-class systems problem for embodied agents. We propose a lifecycle-aware upgrade framework in which every new capability version is treated as a governed deployment candidate rather than an immediately executable replacement. The framework introduces four upgrade compatibility checks -- interface, policy, behavioral, and recovery -- and organizes them into a staged runtime pipeline comprising candidate validation, sandbox evaluation, shadow deployment, gated activation, online monitoring, and rollback.
  We evaluate over 6 rounds of capability upgrade with 15 random seeds. Naive upgrade achieves 72.9% task success but drives unsafe activation to 60% by the final round; governed upgrade retains comparable success (67.4%) while maintaining zero unsafe activations across all rounds (Wilcoxon p=0.003). Shadow deployment reveals 40% of regressions invisible to sandbox evaluation alone, and rollback succeeds in 79.8% of post-activation drift scenarios.

</details>


### [125] [ViVa: A Video-Generative Value Model for Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08168)
*Jindi Lv,Hao Li,Jie Li,Yifei Nie,Fankun Kong,Yang Wang,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Chaojun Ni,Qiuping Deng,Hengtao Li,Jiancheng Lv,Guan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViVa：基于视频生成模型的价值估计方法，通过预测未来本体感觉和标量价值来改进机器人长时域任务中的价值估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的价值模型难以捕捉时间动态，在长时域任务中价值估计不可靠，需要一种能够结合时空先验的价值估计方法

Method: 提出ViVa视频生成价值模型，利用预训练视频生成器，以当前观测和机器人本体感觉为输入，联合预测未来本体感觉和当前状态标量价值

Result: 在真实世界盒子组装任务中显著提升性能，在所有三个任务中产生更可靠的价值信号，准确反映任务进展，并能泛化到新物体

Conclusion: 通过利用视频语料库的时空先验，ViVa展示了视频生成模型在价值估计中的潜力，将价值估计与前瞻性预测相结合

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robot manipulation through large-scale pretraining, but real-world deployment remains challenging due to partial observability and delayed feedback. Reinforcement learning addresses this via value functions, which assess task progress and guide policy improvement. However, existing value models built on vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to capture temporal dynamics, undermining reliable value estimation in long-horizon tasks. In this paper, we propose ViVa, a video-generative value model that repurposes a pretrained video generator for value estimation. Taking the current observation and robot proprioception as input, ViVa jointly predicts future proprioception and a scalar value for the current state. By leveraging the spatiotemporal priors of a pretrained video generator, our approach grounds value estimation in anticipated embodiment dynamics, moving beyond static snapshots to intrinsically couple value with foresight. Integrated into RECAP, ViVa delivers substantial improvements on real-world box assembly. Qualitative analysis across all three tasks confirms that ViVa produces more reliable value signals, accurately reflecting task progress. By leveraging spatiotemporal priors from video corpora, ViVa also generalizes to novel objects, highlighting the promise of video-generative models for value estimation.

</details>


### [126] [State and Trajectory Estimation of Tensegrity Robots via Factor Graphs and Chebyshev Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08185)
*Edgar Granados,Patrick Meng,Charles Tang,Shrimed Sangani,William R. Johnson,Rebecca Kramer-Bottiglio,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于因子图的两阶段方法，用于电缆驱动张拉整体机器人的鲁棒状态/轨迹估计，融合RGB-D相机和电缆长度传感器数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体机器人具有柔顺性和适应性，但其非线性、欠约束动力学使状态估计具有挑战性。可靠的连续时间刚性连杆估计对闭环控制、系统识别和机器学习至关重要，而传统方法往往不足

Method: 提出两阶段方法：在线状态估计采用基于因子图的方法，融合RGB-D相机和电缆长度传感器数据；使用基于马氏距离的聚类算法处理噪声，采用切比雪夫多项式方法估计最可能的速度和中间状态

Result: 与基于ICP的算法相比，该方法在仿真和真实数据上表现良好，能够为复杂的张拉整体机器人运动提供高保真度的连续时间状态和轨迹估计

Conclusion: 这是因子图在该领域的首次应用，因子图利用机器人的结构特性并提供有效的传感器融合解决方案，能够实际处理非线性问题，为张拉整体机器人提供了可靠的状态估计方法

Abstract: Tensegrity robots offer compliance and adaptability, but their nonlinear, and underconstrained dynamics make state estimation challenging. Reliable continuous-time estimation of all rigid links is crucial for closed-loop control, system identification, and machine learning; however, conventional methods often fall short. This paper proposes a two-stage approach for robust state or trajectory estimation (i.e., filtering or smoothing) of a cable-driven tensegrity robot. For online state estimation, this work introduces a factor-graph-based method, which fuses measurements from an RGB-D camera with on-board cable length sensors. To the best of the authors' knowledge, this is the first application of factor graphs in this domain. Factor graphs are a natural choice, as they exploit the robot's structural properties and provide effective sensor fusion solutions capable of handling nonlinearities in practice. Both the Mahalanobis distance-based clustering algorithm, used to handle noise, and the Chebyshev polynomial method, used to estimate the most probable velocities and intermediate states, are shown to perform well on simulated and real-world data, compared to an ICP-based algorithm. Results show that the approach provides high fidelity, continuous-time state and trajectory estimates for complex tensegrity robot motions.

</details>


### [127] [EvoGymCM: Harnessing Continuous Material Stiffness for Soft Robot Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08258)
*Le Shen,Kangyao Huang,Wentao Zhao,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoGymCM是一个软体机器人协同设计基准套件，将连续材料刚度作为与形态和控制并列的一类设计变量，解决了现有平台材料维度离散化限制设计空间的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在软体机器人自动化协同设计中，精确调整材料刚度场以适应任务环境对于发挥其物理潜力至关重要。然而，主流平台（如EvoGym）严格离散化材料维度，人为限制了软体机器人的设计空间和性能。

Method: 提出EvoGymCM基准套件，建立连续材料刚度作为一类设计变量。基于真实世界材料机制，引入两种设置：EvoGymCM-R（反应式，针对可编程材料）和EvoGymCM-I（不变式，针对传统材料）。为解决高维耦合问题，提出两种形态-材料-控制协同设计范式：反应式材料协同设计和不变式材料协同设计。

Result: 系统实验表明，连续材料优化能够提升性能，并在形态、材料和控制之间实现协同效应。

Conclusion: 连续材料刚度作为一类设计变量能够显著提升软体机器人协同设计的性能，并为可编程材料和传统材料提供了有效的设计范式。

Abstract: In the automated co-design of soft robots, precisely adapting the material stiffness field to task environments is crucial for unlocking their full physical potential. However, mainstream platforms (e.g., EvoGym) strictly discretize the material dimension, artificially restricting the design space and performance of soft robots. To address this, we propose EvoGymCM (EvoGym with Continuous Materials), a benchmark suite formally establishing continuous material stiffness as a first-class design variable alongside morphology and control. Aligning with real-world material mechanisms, EvoGymCM introduces two settings: (i) EvoGymCM-R (Reactive), motivated by programmable materials with dynamically tunable stiffness; and (ii) EvoGymCM-I (Invariant), motivated by traditional materials with invariant stiffness fields. To tackle the resulting high-dimensional coupling, we formulate two Morphology-Material-Control co-design paradigms: (i) Reactive-Material Co-Design, which learns real-time stiffness tuning policies to guide programmable materials; and (ii) Invariant-Material Co-Design, which jointly optimizes morphology and fixed material fields to guide traditional material fabrication. Systematic experiments across diverse tasks demonstrate that continuous material optimization boosts performance and unlocks synergy across morphology, material, and control.

</details>


### [128] [EMMa: End-Effector Stability-Oriented Mobile Manipulation for Tracked Rescue Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08292)
*Yifei Wang,Hao Zhang,Jidong Huang,Shuohang Fang,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于履带式移动机械臂的协调运动生成框架，通过耦合末端执行器与移动基座状态的优化模型和隔离控制方案，在复杂救援场景中实现稳定的末端执行器操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在规划和控制层面忽视了末端执行器的多种运动特性，而救援任务中的自主操作不仅需要确保机器人运动可达性和安全性，还需在不同任务需求下维持稳定的末端执行器操作。

Method: 1) 建立耦合末端执行器与移动基座状态的协调路径优化模型，设计紧凑的成本/约束表示以减轻非线性并降低计算复杂度；2) 开发具有前馈补偿和反馈调节的隔离控制方案，实现机器人协调路径跟踪。

Result: 在救援场景的大量仿真和真实世界实验中，所提框架在任务成功率和末端执行器运动稳定性等关键指标上持续优于现有最先进方法，验证了其在复杂移动操作任务中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过协调路径优化和隔离控制，成功解决了履带式移动机械臂在复杂救援场景中实现稳定末端执行器操作的问题，为自主救援机器人提供了有效的运动生成解决方案。

Abstract: The autonomous operation of tracked mobile manipulators in rescue missions requires not only ensuring the reachability and safety of robot motion but also maintaining stable end-effector manipulation under diverse task demands. However, existing studies have overlooked many end-effector motion properties at both the planning and control levels. This paper presents a motion generation framework for tracked mobile manipulators to achieve stable end-effector operation in complex rescue scenarios. The framework formulates a coordinated path optimization model that couples end-effector and mobile base states and designs compact cost/constraint representations to mitigate nonlinearities and reduce computational complexity. Furthermore, an isolated control scheme with feedforward compensation and feedback regulation is developed to enable coordinated path tracking for the robot. Extensive simulated and real-world experiments on rescue scenarios demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms SOTA methods across key metrics, including task success rate and end-effector motion stability, validating its effectiveness and robustness in complex mobile manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [129] [A Unified Multi-Layer Framework for Skill Acquisition from Imperfect Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08341)
*Zi-Qi Yang,Mehrdad R. Kermani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种三层控制框架，通过基于通用机器人柔顺性的学习示教方法，实现高效、直观且安全的技能教学人机交互系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机交互技能教学系统存在碎片化问题，缺乏同时具备高效性、直观性和通用安全性的统一框架。现有方法无法提供既高效又直观且普遍安全的完整解决方案。

Method: 提出三层渐进式控制框架：1）实时学习示教方法，从单次演示中学习轨迹和可变阻抗；2）零空间优化策略，主动管理奇异性并提供一致的交互感受；3）基础零空间柔顺方法，使整个机器人身体能够柔顺适应外部交互而不影响主任务性能。

Result: 在7自由度KUKA LWR机器人上进行了全面的对比实验验证。结果表明，该系统为广泛的人机协作任务提供了更安全、更直观、更高效的一体化解决方案。

Conclusion: 该框架通过三层渐进式设计，将系统转变为多功能人机交互平台，超越了末端执行器特定应用的限制，实现了更安全、直观和高效的人机协作技能教学。

Abstract: Current Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) systems for skill teaching are fragmented, and existing approaches in the literature do not offer a cohesive framework that is simultaneously efficient, intuitive, and universally safe. This paper presents a novel, layered control framework that addresses this fundamental gap by enabling robust, compliant Learning from Demonstration (LfD) built upon a foundation of universal robot compliance. The proposed approach is structured in three progressive and interconnected stages. First, we introduce a real-time LfD method that learns both the trajectory and variable impedance from a single demonstration, significantly improving efficiency and reproduction fidelity. To ensure high-quality and intuitive {kinesthetic teaching}, we then present a null-space optimization strategy that proactively manages singularities and provides a consistent interaction feel during human demonstration. Finally, to ensure generalized safety, we introduce a foundational null-space compliance method that enables the entire robot body to compliantly adapt to post-learning external interactions without compromising main task performance. This final contribution transforms the system into a versatile HRI platform, moving beyond end-effector (EE)-specific applications. We validate the complete framework through comprehensive comparative experiments on a 7-DOF KUKA LWR robot. The results demonstrate a safer, more intuitive, and more efficient unified system for a wide range of human-robot collaborative tasks.

</details>


### [130] [Sumo: Dynamic and Generalizable Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08508)
*John Z. Zhang,Maks Sorokin,Jan Brüdigam,Brandon Hung,Stephen Phillips,Dmitry Yershov,Farzad Niroui,Tong Zhao,Leonor Fermoselle,Xinghao Zhu,Chao Cao,Duy Ta,Tao Pang,Jiuguang Wang,Preston Culbertson,Zachary Manchester,Simon Le Cléac'h*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种sim-to-real方法，通过测试时引导预训练全身控制策略与采样规划器结合，使腿式机器人能够动态操控大型重物


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决腿式机器人动态操控大型重物时面临的挑战，传统方法难以处理这类需要全身协调的复杂操作任务

Method: 结合预训练的全身控制策略与采样规划器，在测试时进行引导，无需额外调参或训练，可通过调整成本函数增强性能

Result: 在Spot四足机器人上成功完成多种挑战性任务，包括扶正超过机器人额定举重能力的轮胎、拖动比机器人更大更高的护栏；在仿真中扩展到人形机器人开门、推桌等任务

Conclusion: 该方法展示了sim-to-real方法在腿式机器人动态操控任务中的有效性，具有良好泛化能力，为复杂机器人操作任务提供了新解决方案

Abstract: This paper presents a sim-to-real approach that enables legged robots to dynamically manipulate large and heavy objects with whole-body dexterity. Our key insight is that by performing test-time steering of a pre-trained whole-body control policy with a sample-based planner, we can enable these robots to solve a variety of dynamic loco-manipulation tasks. Interestingly, we find our method generalizes to a diverse set of objects and tasks with no additional tuning or training, and can be further enhanced by flexibly adjusting the cost function at test time. We demonstrate the capabilities of our approach through a variety of challenging loco-manipulation tasks on a Spot quadruped robot in the real world, including uprighting a tire heavier than the robot's nominal lifting capacity and dragging a crowd-control barrier larger and taller than the robot itself. Additionally, we show that the same approach can be generalized to humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, such as opening a door and pushing a table, in simulation. Project code and videos are available at \href{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/}{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/}.

</details>


### [131] [ActiveGlasses: Learning Manipulation with Active Vision from Ego-centric Human Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08534)
*Yanwen Zou,Chenyang Shi,Wenye Yu,Han Xue,Jun Lv,Ye Pan,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveGlasses系统通过智能眼镜上的立体相机采集人类裸手操作的第一人称演示，并利用相同的相机在机器人上复现人类主动视觉，实现零样本迁移的机器人操作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据采集方法依赖专用手持设备，增加了操作者负担、限制了可扩展性，且难以捕捉人类日常交互中自然协调的感知-操作行为。需要一种更自然的系统，既能忠实记录人类操作和感知行为，又能实现到机器人平台的零样本迁移。

Method: 使用智能眼镜上的立体相机作为唯一感知设备：操作者佩戴进行裸手演示，同一相机部署在6自由度感知臂上复现人类主动视觉。从演示中提取物体轨迹，采用以物体为中心的点云策略联合预测操作和头部运动。

Result: 在涉及遮挡和精确交互的多个挑战性任务中，ActiveGlasses实现了带主动视觉的零样本迁移，在相同硬件设置下持续优于强基线方法，并在两个机器人平台上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ActiveGlasses系统通过第一人称人类演示和主动视觉复现，提供了一种自然、可扩展的机器人操作学习方法，成功解决了现有数据采集方法的局限性，实现了零样本迁移和跨平台泛化。

Abstract: Large-scale real-world robot data collection is a prerequisite for bringing robots into everyday deployment. However, existing pipelines often rely on specialized handheld devices to bridge the embodiment gap, which not only increases operator burden and limits scalability, but also makes it difficult to capture the naturally coordinated perception-manipulation behaviors of human daily interaction. This challenge calls for a more natural system that can faithfully capture human manipulation and perception behaviors while enabling zero-shot transfer to robotic platforms. We introduce ActiveGlasses, a system for learning robot manipulation from ego-centric human demonstrations with active vision. A stereo camera mounted on smart glasses serves as the sole perception device for both data collection and policy inference: the operator wears it during bare-hand demonstrations, and the same camera is mounted on a 6-DoF perception arm during deployment to reproduce human active vision. To enable zero-transfer, we extract object trajectories from demonstrations and use an object-centric point-cloud policy to jointly predict manipulation and head movement. Across several challenging tasks involving occlusion and precise interaction, ActiveGlasses achieves zero-shot transfer with active vision, consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same hardware setup, and generalizes across two robot platforms.

</details>


### [132] [Fail2Drive: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Driving Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08535)
*Simon Gerstenecker,Andreas Geiger,Katrin Renz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fail2Drive是首个用于CARLA中闭环自动驾驶泛化的配对路线基准测试，包含200条路线和17种新场景类别，通过隔离分布偏移效应将定性失败转化为定量诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶基准测试通常重用训练场景进行测试，导致成功可能反映的是记忆而非鲁棒驾驶行为，缺乏对真实泛化能力的衡量。分布偏移下的泛化仍然是闭环自动驾驶的核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出Fail2Drive基准测试，包含200条路线和17种新场景类别，涵盖外观、布局、行为和鲁棒性偏移。每条偏移路线都匹配一个分布内对应路线，以隔离偏移效应。提供开源工具箱用于创建新场景并通过特权专家策略验证可解性。

Result: 评估多个最先进模型显示一致的性能下降，平均成功率下降22.8%。分析揭示了意外失败模式，如忽略LiDAR中清晰可见的物体，以及未能学习自由空间和占用空间的基本概念。

Conclusion: Fail2Drive为基准测试和改进闭环驾驶泛化建立了可重复的基础。所有代码、数据和工具均已开源，加速后续研究工作。

Abstract: Generalization under distribution shift remains a central bottleneck for closed-loop autonomous driving. Although simulators like CARLA enable safe and scalable testing, existing benchmarks rarely measure true generalization: they typically reuse training scenarios at test time. Success can therefore reflect memorization rather than robust driving behavior. We introduce Fail2Drive, the first paired-route benchmark for closed-loop generalization in CARLA, with 200 routes and 17 new scenario classes spanning appearance, layout, behavioral, and robustness shifts. Each shifted route is matched with an in-distribution counterpart, isolating the effect of the shift and turning qualitative failures into quantitative diagnostics. Evaluating multiple state-of-the-art models reveals consistent degradation, with an average success-rate drop of 22.8\%. Our analysis uncovers unexpected failure modes, such as ignoring objects clearly visible in the LiDAR and failing to learn the fundamental concepts of free and occupied space. To accelerate follow-up work, Fail2Drive includes an open-source toolbox for creating new scenarios and validating solvability via a privileged expert policy. Together, these components establish a reproducible foundation for benchmarking and improving closed-loop driving generalization. We open-source all code, data, and tools at https://github.com/autonomousvision/fail2drive .

</details>


### [133] [SIM1: Physics-Aligned Simulator as Zero-Shot Data Scaler in Deformable Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08544)
*Yunsong Zhou,Hangxu Liu,Xuekun Jiang,Xing Shen,Yuanzhen Zhou,Hui Wang,Baole Fang,Yang Tian,Mulin Yu,Qiaojun Yu,Li Ma,Hengjie Li,Hanqing Wang,Jia Zeng,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SIM1：基于物理对齐的真实-仿真-真实数据引擎，通过将仿真与物理世界对齐，为可变形物体操作提供可扩展的监督学习，显著减少真实数据需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体操作需要大量数据，现有仿真到真实（sim-to-real）方法基于刚体抽象，导致几何不匹配、软体动力学脆弱、运动基元不适合布料交互。仿真失败的原因不是其合成性质，而是缺乏物理基础。

Method: SIM1：物理对齐的真实-仿真-真实数据引擎。通过有限演示将场景数字化为度量一致的双胞胎，通过弹性建模校准可变形动力学，使用基于扩散的轨迹生成和质量过滤扩展行为，将稀疏观察转化为具有接近演示保真度的规模化合成监督。

Result: 在纯合成数据上训练的策略在1:15的等价比下达到与真实数据基线相当的性能，在真实世界部署中实现90%的零样本成功率和50%的泛化提升。

Conclusion: 物理对齐的仿真可作为可变形物体操作的可扩展监督学习方式，为数据高效策略学习提供实用途径。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation with deformable objects represents a data-intensive regime in embodied learning, where shape, contact, and topology co-evolve in ways that far exceed the variability of rigids. Although simulation promises relief from the cost of real-world data acquisition, prevailing sim-to-real pipelines remain rooted in rigid-body abstractions, producing mismatched geometry, fragile soft dynamics, and motion primitives poorly suited for cloth interaction. We posit that simulation fails not for being synthetic, but for being ungrounded. To address this, we introduce SIM1, a physics-aligned real-to-sim-to-real data engine that grounds simulation in the physical world. Given limited demonstrations, the system digitizes scenes into metric-consistent twins, calibrates deformable dynamics through elastic modeling, and expands behaviors via diffusion-based trajectory generation with quality filtering. This pipeline transforms sparse observations into scaled synthetic supervision with near-demonstration fidelity. Experiments show that policies trained on purely synthetic data achieve parity with real-data baselines at a 1:15 equivalence ratio, while delivering 90% zero-shot success and 50% generalization gains in real-world deployment. These results validate physics-aligned simulation as scalable supervision for deformable manipulation and a practical pathway for data-efficient policy learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [An Analysis of Artificial Intelligence Adoption in NIH-Funded Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07424)
*Navapat Nananukul,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究使用大语言模型分析NIH资助的58,746个生物医学研究项目，发现AI项目占15.9%且获得13.4%的资金溢价，但存在研究到部署的严重差距，且健康差异研究代表性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解NIH资助组合中人工智能和机器学习采用情况对研究资助策略、机构规划和卫生政策至关重要。大语言模型的出现为大规模分析非结构化研究文档提供了新方法。

Method: 采用人机协同研究方法，利用大语言模型自动分类和总结大规模研究描述。分析了58,746个2025年NIH资助的生物医学研究项目。

Result: 1. AI项目占NIH组合的15.9%，获得13.4%资金溢价，集中在发现、预测和数据整合领域；2. 存在严重的研究到部署差距，79%的AI项目处于研究/开发阶段，仅14.7%涉及临床部署；3. 健康差异研究严重不足，仅占AI资助工作的5.7%。

Conclusion: 研究结果为基于证据的政策干预提供了框架，以调整NIH的AI投资组合，使其与健康公平目标和战略研究重点保持一致。

Abstract: Understanding the landscape of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) adoption across the National Institutes of Health (NIH) portfolio is critical for research funding strategy, institutional planning, and health policy. The advent of large language models (LLMs) has fundamentally transformed research landscape analysis, enabling researchers to perform large-scale semantic extraction from thousands of unstructured research documents. In this paper, we illustrate a human-in-the-loop research methodology for LLMs to automatically classify and summarize research descriptions at scale. Using our methodology, we present a comprehensive analysis of 58,746 NIH-funded biomedical research projects from 2025. We show that: (1) AI constitutes 15.9% of the NIH portfolio with a 13.4% funding premium, concentrated in discovery, prediction, and data integration across disease domains; (2) a critical research-to-deployment gap exists, with 79% of AI projects remaining in research/development stages while only 14.7% engage in clinical deployment or implementation; and (3) health disparities research is severely underrepresented at just 5.7% of AI-funded work despite its importance to NIH's equity mission. These findings establish a framework for evidence-based policy interventions to align the NIH AI portfolio with health equity goals and strategic research priorities.

</details>


### [135] [ConsistRM: Improving Generative Reward Models via Consistency-Aware Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07484)
*Yu Liang,Liangxin Liu,Longzheng Wang,Yan Wang,Yueyang Zhang,Long Xia,Zhiyuan Sun,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConsistRM是一个无需人工标注的自训练框架，通过一致性感知奖励机制解决生成式奖励模型的数据依赖和训练不稳定问题，在多个基准上平均优于传统强化学习微调1.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型虽然比传统标量奖励模型具有更强的表示能力和灵活性，但面临两大挑战：1) 依赖昂贵的人工标注数据限制了可扩展性；2) 自训练方法通常存在不稳定性和易受奖励攻击的脆弱性。

Method: 提出ConsistRM自训练框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 一致性感知答案奖励：产生具有时间一致性的可靠伪标签，提供更稳定的模型优化；2) 一致性感知批评奖励：评估多个批评之间的语义一致性，分配细粒度和差异化的奖励。

Result: 在5个基准数据集和4个基础模型上的实验表明，ConsistRM平均优于传统强化学习微调1.5%。进一步分析显示，该方法增强了输出一致性，并缓解了由输入顺序引起的位置偏差。

Conclusion: ConsistRM通过一致性感知奖励机制有效解决了GRM训练中的关键问题，证明了在无需人工标注的情况下实现稳定高效训练的可能性，一致性感知奖励在改进生成式奖励模型方面具有显著效果。

Abstract: Generative reward models (GRMs) have emerged as a promising approach for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences by offering greater representational capacity and flexibility than traditional scalar reward models. However, GRMs face two major challenges: reliance on costly human-annotated data restricts scalability, and self-training approaches often suffer from instability and vulnerability to reward hacking. To address these issues, we propose ConsistRM, a self-training framework that enables effective and stable GRM training without human annotations. ConsistRM incorporates the Consistency-Aware Answer Reward, which produces reliable pseudo-labels with temporal consistency, thereby providing more stable model optimization. Moreover, the Consistency-Aware Critique Reward is introduced to assess semantic consistency across multiple critiques and allocates fine-grained and differentiated rewards. Experiments on five benchmark datasets across four base models demonstrate that ConsistRM outperforms vanilla Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) by an average of 1.5%. Further analysis shows that ConsistRM enhances output consistency and mitigates position bias caused by input order, highlighting the effectiveness of consistency-aware rewards in improving GRMs.

</details>


### [136] [ReflectRM: Boosting Generative Reward Models via Self-Reflection within a Unified Judgment Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07506)
*Kai Qin,Liangxin Liu,Yu Liang,Longzheng Wang,Yan Wang,Yueyang Zhang,Long Xia,Zhiyuan Sun,Houde Liu,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReflectRM是一种新型生成式奖励模型，通过自我反思机制评估分析过程质量，在统一生成框架下联合建模响应偏好和分析偏好，显著提升对齐性能并减少位置偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式奖励模型主要关注结果级监督，忽视了分析过程质量，这限制了它们的潜力。需要一种能够评估分析质量并增强偏好建模的方法。

Method: 提出ReflectRM，利用自我反思评估分析质量，在统一生成框架下联合建模响应偏好和分析偏好。推理时使用自我反思能力识别最可靠的分析，从中得出最终偏好预测。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，ReflectRM持续提升性能，在Qwen3-4B上平均准确率增益+3.7。响应偏好和分析偏好相互增强，显著缓解位置偏差，相比领先GRMs提升+10.2，成为更稳定的评估器。

Conclusion: ReflectRM通过自我反思机制有效提升生成式奖励模型的性能，联合建模响应和分析偏好，显著减少位置偏差，为LLM对齐提供了更可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: Reward Models (RMs) are critical components in the Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pipeline, directly determining the alignment quality of Large Language Models (LLMs). Recently, Generative Reward Models (GRMs) have emerged as a superior paradigm, offering higher interpretability and stronger generalization than traditional scalar RMs. However, existing methods for GRMs focus primarily on outcome-level supervision, neglecting analytical process quality, which constrains their potential. To address this, we propose ReflectRM, a novel GRM that leverages self-reflection to assess analytical quality and enhance preference modeling. ReflectRM is trained under a unified generative framework for joint modeling of response preference and analysis preference. During inference, we use its self-reflection capability to identify the most reliable analysis, from which the final preference prediction is derived. Experiments across four benchmarks show that ReflectRM consistently improves performance, achieving an average accuracy gain of +3.7 on Qwen3-4B. Further experiments confirm that response preference and analysis preference are mutually reinforcing. Notably, ReflectRM substantially mitigates positional bias, yielding +10.2 improvement compared with leading GRMs and establishing itself as a more stable evaluator.

</details>


### [137] [Trust the AI, Doubt Yourself: The Effect of Urgency on Self-Confidence in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07535)
*Baran Shajari,Xiaoran Liu,Kyanna Dagenais,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现AI交互中的紧迫感会损害用户自信，虽然不影响对AI的信任，但可能导致长期性能下降和不可持续的系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人机交互中被忽视的紧迫感因素，探讨AI提示用户时的紧迫感如何影响用户心理状态和系统可持续性

Method: 通过30名参与者的实验，比较有准备和无准备情况下用户接触人机交互设置时的反应差异

Result: 紧迫感不影响对AI的信任，但会损害用户自信和自我效能感；有准备地接触人机交互设置的用户表现出更高自信

Conclusion: 紧迫感可能导致长期性能损失、次优决策和人为错误，最终影响AI系统可持续性；建议软件工程师和决策者关注用户准备过程

Abstract: Studies show that interactions with an AI system fosters trust in human users towards AI. An often overlooked element of such interaction dynamics is the (sense of) urgency when the human user is prompted by an AI agent, e.g., for advice or guidance. In this paper, we show that although the presence of urgency in human-AI interactions does not affect the trust in AI, it may be detrimental to the human user's self-confidence and self-efficacy. In the long run, the loss of confidence may lead to performance loss, suboptimal decisions, human errors, and ultimately, unsustainable AI systems. Our evidence comes from an experiment with 30 human participants. Our results indicate that users may feel more confident in their work when they are eased into the human-AI setup rather than exposed to it without preparation. We elaborate on the implications of this finding for software engineers and decision-makers.

</details>


### [138] [Dual-Loop Control in DCVerse: Advancing Reliable Deployment of AI in Data Centers via Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07559)
*Qingang Zhang,Yuejun Yan,Guangyu Wu,Siew-Chien Wong,Jimin Jia,Zhaoyang Wang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于数字孪生的双环控制框架DLCF，解决数据中心能效与可靠性平衡问题，通过数字孪生、策略库和双环机制实现安全高效的DRL部署，在实际冷却系统中验证了4.09%的节能效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据中心规模与复杂性不断增加，在能效与宕机风险之间取得平衡面临重大挑战。深度强化学习虽具潜力，但在关键任务系统中部署受限，主要由于数据稀缺和缺乏实时预评估机制。

Method: 提出双环控制框架DLCF，包含三个核心实体：物理系统、数字孪生和多样化DRL策略库。通过双环机制实现实时数据采集、数据同化、DRL策略训练、预评估和专家验证的闭环流程。

Result: 理论分析表明DLCF能提升样本效率、泛化能力、安全性和最优性。基于DLCF实现DCVerse平台，在实际数据中心冷却系统案例验证中，相比传统控制策略实现最高4.09%的节能，且不违反SLA要求，同时提升策略可解释性和DRL部署可信度。

Conclusion: DLCF为数据中心可靠的AI控制提供了基础框架，支持更可信的DRL部署，未来可扩展至全系统优化，为关键基础设施的智能控制开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The growing scale and complexity of modern data centers present major challenges in balancing energy efficiency with outage risk. Although Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) shows strong potential for intelligent control, its deployment in mission-critical systems is limited by data scarcity and the lack of real-time pre-evaluation mechanisms. This paper introduces the Dual-Loop Control Framework (DLCF), a digital twin-based architecture designed to overcome these challenges. The framework comprises three core entities: the physical system, a digital twin, and a policy reservoir of diverse DRL agents. These components interact through a dual-loop mechanism involving real-time data acquisition, data assimilation, DRL policy training, pre-evaluation, and expert verification. Theoretical analysis shows how DLCF can improve sample efficiency, generalization, safety, and optimality. Leveraging DLCF, we implemented the DCVerse platform and validated it through case studies on a real-world data center cooling system. The evaluation shows that our approach achieves up to 4.09% energy savings over conventional control strategies without violating SLA requirements. Additionally, the framework improves policy interpretability and supports more trustworthy DRL deployment. This work provides a foundation for reliable AI-based control in data centers and points toward future extensions for holistic, system-wide optimization.

</details>


### [139] [From Papers to Property Tables: A Priority-Based LLM Workflow for Materials Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07584)
*Koushik Rameshbabu,Jing Luo,Ali Shargh,Khalid A. El-Awady,Jaafar A. El-Awady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型的层次化工作流，自动从科研论文全文中提取和重构结构化、弹道级别的冲击物理实验记录，以合金层裂强度为案例，实现高精度数据提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据分散在科研论文的文本、表格和图表中，报告方式不一致，导致手动数据提取和聚合过程缓慢且容易出错，需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 采用提示驱动的层次化工作流，使用大语言模型从全文科研论文中提取信息。工作流包含三个优先级策略：T1从文本/表格直接提取，T2基于物理公式推导，T3从图表数字化提取。提取的值进行单位归一化、优先级标记，并通过物理一致性和合理性检查进行验证。

Result: 在30篇已发表科研论文的基准测试中（包含11,967个评估数据点），工作流总体准确率达到94.69%。优先级准确率分别为：T1 94.93%、T2 92.04%、T3 83.49%。跨模型测试显示文本/表格和公式推导字段具有强一致性，图表提取一致性较低。API接口实现证明了方法的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作流展示了将非结构化技术文献转换为可追溯、分析就绪数据集的实用方法，无需任务特定的微调，能够在材料科学中实现可扩展的数据库构建。

Abstract: Scientific data are widely dispersed across research articles and are often reported inconsistently across text, tables, and figures, making manual data extraction and aggregation slow and error-prone. We present a prompt-driven, hierarchical workflow that uses a large language model (LLM) to automatically extract and reconstruct structured, shot-level shock-physics experimental records by integrating information distributed across text, tables, figures, and physics-based derivations from full-text published research articles, using alloy spall strength as a representative case study. The pipeline targeted 37 experimentally relevant fields per shot and applied a three-level priority strategy: (T1) direct extraction from text/tables, (T2) physics-based derivation using verified governing relations, and (T3) digitization from figures when necessary. Extracted values were normalized to canonical units, tagged by priority for traceability, and validated with physics-based consistency and plausibility checks. Evaluated on a benchmark of 30 published research articles comprising 11,967 evaluated data points, the workflow achieved high overall accuracy, with priority-wise accuracies of 94.93% (T1), 92.04% (T2), and 83.49% (T3), and an overall weighted accuracy of 94.69%. Cross-model testing further indicated strong agreement for text/table and equation-derived fields, with lower agreement for figure-based extraction. Implementation through an API interface demonstrated the scalability of the approach, achieving consistent extraction performance and, in a subset of test cases, matching or exceeding chat-based accuracy. This workflow demonstrates a practical approach for converting unstructured technical literature into traceable, analysis-ready datasets without task-specific fine-tuning, enabling scalable database construction in materials science.

</details>


### [140] [Too long; didn't solve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07593)
*Lucía M. Cabrera,Isaac Saxton-Knight*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究发现数学问题提示长度和解答长度与大型语言模型失败率呈正相关，结构长度与经验难度相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学基准测试广泛用于评估大语言模型的推理能力，但结构特性如何影响模型行为尚不清楚。本研究旨在探究提示长度和解答长度这两个结构变量与模型性能的关系

Method: 构建专家编写的对抗性数学问题数据集，分析提示长度和解答长度两个结构变量与模型性能的关系，并进行跨模型分歧的探索性分析

Result: 发现提示长度和解答长度都与模型失败率呈正相关。在难度调整的标准化分析中，两个变量与实现的模型分离保持弱负相关，提示长度的相关性稍强

Conclusion: 主要稳健发现是：在该数据集中，结构长度与经验难度相关，提示长度和解答长度都会增加模型失败的可能性

Abstract: Mathematical benchmarks consisting of a range of mathematics problems are widely used to evaluate the reasoning abilities of large language models, yet little is known about how their structural properties influence model behaviour. In this work, we investigate two structural length variables, prompt length and solution length, and analyse how they relate to model performance on a newly constructed adversarial dataset of expert-authored mathematics problems. We find that both prompt and solution lengths correlate positively with increased model failure across models. We also include a secondary, exploratory analysis of cross-model disagreement. Under a difficulty-adjusted normalised analysis, both variables retain weak negative associations with realised model separation, slightly stronger for prompt length. Overall, our main robust finding is that structural length is linked to empirical difficulty in this dataset.

</details>


### [141] [Reasoning Graphs: Deterministic Agent Accuracy through Evidence-Centric Chain-of-Thought Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07595)
*Matthew Penaroza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出推理图和检索图，通过图结构持久化智能体的推理链，实现证据中心反馈，无需重新训练即可提升语言模型智能体的准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体每次查询都从零开始推理，丢弃之前的推理链，导致准确率低、方差高，相似查询可能随机成功或失败。需要一种机制来持久化推理过程并实现证据层面的反馈。

Method: 引入推理图和检索图：推理图将智能体对每个证据的推理链持久化为结构化边；检索图为管道规划器提供候选集筛选。通过图遍历实现证据中心反馈，无需重新训练基础模型。

Result: 系统形成自我改进的反馈循环：随着连续运行，准确率上升，方差下降。所有改进都来自图遍历的上下文工程，基础模型保持冻结状态。

Conclusion: 推理图和检索图提供了一种结构化的方法来持久化推理过程，实现证据中心反馈，显著提升语言模型智能体在多跳问答任务中的准确性和稳定性，且完全可追溯。

Abstract: Language model agents reason from scratch on every query: each time an agent retrieves evidence and deliberates, the chain of thought is discarded and the next similar query starts with no prior insight. This produces lower accuracy and high variance, as the same type of query can succeed or fail unpredictably. We introduce reasoning graphs, a graph structure that persists an agent's per-evidence chain of thought as structured edges connected to the evidence items they evaluate. Unlike prior memory mechanisms that store distilled strategies as flat records indexed by query similarity or appended by recency, reasoning graphs enable evidence-centric feedback: given a new candidate set, the system traverses all incoming evaluation edges for each evidence item across all prior runs, surfacing how that specific item has been judged before. This backward traversal from evidence inward is a structurally different capability from query-similarity retrieval, because the feedback is tied to the specific evidence the agent is currently examining, not to the query. We further introduce retrieval graphs, a complementary structure that feeds a pipeline planner to tighten the candidate funnel over successive runs. Together, both graphs form a self-improving feedback loop: accuracy rises and variance collapses over successive runs, with every decision fully traceable through the graph. This improvement requires no retraining; the base model remains frozen and all gains come from context engineering via graph traversal. We formalize the graph structure, traversal algorithms, and feedback mechanisms, and describe a sequential cluster evaluation protocol for measuring accuracy convergence and variance collapse on multi-hop question answering benchmarks.

</details>


### [142] [How Independent are Large Language Models? A Statistical Framework for Auditing Behavioral Entanglement and Reweighting Verifier Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07650)
*Chenchen Kuai,Jiwan Jiang,Zihao Zhu,Hao Wang,Keshu Wu,Zihao Li,Yunlong Zhang,Chenxi Liu,Zhengzhong Tu,Zhiwen Fan,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种统计框架来审计黑盒大语言模型之间的行为纠缠问题，揭示了看似独立的模型实际上存在隐藏的依赖关系，并开发了去纠缠的验证器集成重加权方法来改善性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生态系统的快速增长引发了一个关键问题：看似多样的模型是否真正独立？共享的预训练数据、知识蒸馏和对齐流程可能导致隐藏的行为依赖关系（潜在纠缠），这会破坏多模型系统（如LLM-as-a-judge流水线和集成验证）的性能，因为这些系统隐含地假设了独立信号。在实践中，这表现为相关的推理模式和同步失败，表面上的共识反映了共享的错误模式而非独立验证。

Method: 开发了一个统计框架来审计黑盒LLM之间的行为纠缠。该方法引入了多分辨率层次结构，通过两个信息论指标来表征联合失败流形：1）难度加权行为纠缠指数，放大简单任务上的同步失败；2）累积信息增益指标，捕捉错误响应的方向性对齐。在来自六个模型家族的18个LLM上进行了广泛实验，分析了行为纠缠对LLM-as-a-judge评估的影响。

Result: 实验发现了广泛的行为纠缠现象。CIG指标与评判器精度下降存在统计显著关联：GPT-4o-mini的Spearman系数为0.64（p < 0.001），Llama3-based评判器为0.71（p < 0.01），表明更强的依赖关系对应着增加的过度认可偏差。通过去纠缠的验证器集成重加权，基于推断的独立性调整模型贡献，可以减轻相关偏差并提高验证性能，相比多数投票获得了高达4.5%的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 大语言模型之间存在广泛的行为纠缠，这会破坏多模型系统的可靠性。提出的统计框架能够有效审计这种纠缠，而基于去纠缠的集成重加权方法可以显著改善验证性能。这项工作强调了在构建依赖多个LLM的系统时考虑模型间依赖关系的重要性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of the large language model (LLM) ecosystem raises a critical question: are seemingly diverse models truly independent? Shared pretraining data, distillation, and alignment pipelines can induce hidden behavioral dependencies, latent entanglement, that undermine multi-model systems such as LLM-as-a-judge pipelines and ensemble verification, which implicitly assume independent signals. In practice, this manifests as correlated reasoning patterns and synchronized failures, where apparent agreement reflects shared error modes rather than independent validation. To address this, we develop a statistical framework for auditing behavioral entanglement among black-box LLMs. Our approach introduces a multi-resolution hierarchy that characterizes the joint failure manifold through two information-theoretic metrics: (i) a Difficulty-Weighted Behavioral Entanglement Index, which amplifies synchronized failures on easy tasks, and (ii) a Cumulative Information Gain (CIG) metric, which captures directional alignment in erroneous responses. Through extensive experiments on 18 LLMs from six model families, we identify widespread behavioral entanglement and analyze its impact on LLM-as-a-judge evaluation. We find that CIG exhibits a statistically significant association with degradation in judge precision, with Spearman coefficient of 0.64 (p < 0.001) for GPT-4o-mini and 0.71 (p < 0.01) for Llama3-based judges, indicating that stronger dependency corresponds to increased over-endorsement bias. Finally, we demonstrate a practical use case of entanglement through de-entangled verifier ensemble reweighting. By adjusting model contributions based on inferred independence, the proposed method mitigates correlated bias and improves verification performance, achieving up to a 4.5% accuracy gain over majority voting.

</details>


### [143] [Bridging Natural Language and Interactive What-If Interfaces via LLM-Generated Declarative Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07652)
*Sneha Gathani,Sirui Zeng,Diya Patel,Ryan Rossi,Dan Marshall,Cagatay Demiralp,Steven Drucker,Zhicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段工作流，将自然语言假设分析问题转换为交互式可视化界面，使用中间表示（Praxa规范语言）提高可靠性和一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假设分析工具存在不足：电子表格和BI工具设置繁琐耗时，基于LLM的聊天界面语义脆弱、容易误解意图且结果不一致，需要更可靠的交互式假设分析解决方案

Method: 两阶段工作流：1) LLM将自然语言问题转换为PSL规范，支持验证和修复错误；2) 将规范编译为带参数控制和联动可视化的交互式界面。使用405个问题、11种分析类型、5个数据集和3个LLM进行基准测试

Result: 基准测试显示52.42%的规范无需干预即可正确生成。分析失败案例得出错误分类学（非功能性和功能性错误），通过少量样本提示进行针对性修复，成功率提升至80.42%。证明中间规范对可靠连接自然语言和交互界面至关重要

Conclusion: 中间规范表示是LLM驱动的假设分析系统中可靠连接自然语言和交互界面的关键，能够检测和修复错误，避免生成看似合理但误导性的界面

Abstract: What-if analysis (WIA) is an iterative, multi-step process where users explore and compare hypothetical scenarios by adjusting parameters, applying constraints, and scoping data through interactive interfaces. Current tools fall short of supporting effective interactive WIA: spreadsheet and BI tools require time-consuming and laborious setup, while LLM-based chatbot interfaces are semantically fragile, frequently misinterpret intent, and produce inconsistent results as conversations progress. To address these limitations, we present a two-stage workflow that translates natural language (NL) WIA questions into interactive visual interfaces via an intermediate representation, powered by the Praxa Specification Language (PSL): first, LLMs generate PSL specifications from NL questions capturing analytical intent and logic, enabling validation and repair of erroneous specifications; and second, the specifications are compiled into interactive visual interfaces with parameter controls and linked visualizations. We benchmark this workflow with 405 WIA questions spanning 11 WIA types, 5 datasets, and 3 state-of-the-art LLMs. The results show that across models, half of specifications (52.42%) are generated correctly without intervention. We perform an analysis of the failure cases and derive an error taxonomy spanning non-functional errors (specifications fail to compile) and functional errors (specifications compile but misrepresent intent). Based on the taxonomy, we apply targeted repairs on the failure cases using few-shot prompts and improve the success rate to 80.42%. Finally, we show how undetected functional errors propagate through compilation into plausible but misleading interfaces, demonstrating that the intermediate specification is critical for reliably bridging NL and interactive WIA interface in LLM-powered WIA systems.

</details>


### [144] [From Debate to Decision: Conformal Social Choice for Safe Multi-Agent Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07667)
*Mengdie Flora Wang,Haochen Xie,Guanghui Wang,Aijing Gao,Guang Yang,Ziyuan Li,Qucy Wei Qiu,Fangwei Han,Hengzhi Qiu,Yajing Huang,Bing Zhu,Jae Oh Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Conformal Social Choice方法，通过后处理决策层将多智能体辩论输出转化为经过校准的"行动vs升级"决策，确保在错误共识情况下不自动执行错误答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论虽然能提升LLM推理能力，但智能体间的共识并不代表答案正确。当智能体通过社会强化收敛到错误答案时，基于共识的停止机制会将错误提交给自动化操作且无法补救。需要一种方法来防止在错误共识情况下自动执行错误答案。

Method: 提出Conformal Social Choice后处理决策层：1) 从异构智能体收集语言化的概率分布；2) 通过线性意见池聚合这些分布；3) 使用分割保形预测进行校准，生成具有边际覆盖保证的预测集；4) 采用分层行动策略：单元素集对应自主行动，多元素集对应人工升级。

Result: 在8个MMLU-Pro领域使用三个智能体(Claude Haiku, DeepSeek-R1, Qwen-3 32B)的实验中，覆盖度保持在目标值的1-2个百分点内。关键发现不是辩论变得更准确，而是保形层使其失败变得可操作：在α=0.05时，81.9%的错误共识案例被拦截。由于该层拒绝在辩论自信错误的情况下行动，剩余的保形单元素集达到90.0-96.8%的准确率(比共识停止高出22.1个百分点)。

Conclusion: Conformal Social Choice通过将多智能体辩论输出转化为经过校准的决策，解决了错误共识导致自动化错误行动的问题。该方法提供了边际覆盖保证，使辩论失败变得可操作，代价是降低了自动化程度，但操作点可通过α参数由用户调整。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate improves LLM reasoning, yet agreement among agents is not evidence of correctness. When agents converge on a wrong answer through social reinforcement, consensus-based stopping commits that error to an automated action with no recourse. We introduce Conformal Social Choice, a post-hoc decision layer that converts debate outputs into calibrated act-versus-escalate decisions. Verbalized probability distributions from heterogeneous agents are aggregated via a linear opinion pool and calibrated with split conformal prediction, yielding prediction sets with a marginal coverage guarantee: the correct answer is included with probability ${\geq}\,1{-}α$, without assumptions on individual model calibration. A hierarchical action policy maps singleton sets to autonomous action and larger sets to human escalation. On eight MMLU-Pro domains with three agents (Claude Haiku, DeepSeek-R1, Qwen-3 32B), coverage stays within 1--2 points of the target. The key finding is not that debate becomes more accurate, but that the conformal layer makes its failures actionable: 81.9% of wrong-consensus cases are intercepted at $α{=}0.05$. Because the layer refuses to act on cases where debate is confidently wrong, the remaining conformal singletons reach 90.0--96.8% accuracy (up to 22.1pp above consensus stopping) -- a selection effect, not a reasoning improvement. This safety comes at the cost of automation, but the operating point is user-adjustable via $α$.

</details>


### [145] [Multi-Agent Orchestration for High-Throughput Materials Screening on a Leadership-Class System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07681)
*Thang Duc Pham,Harikrishna Tummalapalli,Fakhrul Hasan Bhuiyan,Álvaro Vázquez Mayagoitia,Christine Simpson,Riccardo Balin,Venkatram Vishwanath,Murat Keçeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展的分层多智能体框架，用于在HPC系统上协调高通量筛选工作流，解决LLM智能体大规模部署时的串行瓶颈问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与HPC的融合正在将科学工作流从人工导向转变为自适应系统，但LLM智能体的大规模部署面临挑战。单智能体架构和顺序工具调用在执行大规模模拟活动时成为串行瓶颈，无法充分利用百亿亿次计算资源的并行性。

Method: 提出可扩展的分层多智能体框架，采用规划者-执行者架构：中央规划智能体动态划分工作负载并将子任务分配给并行执行智能体群。所有执行智能体通过共享的模型上下文协议(MCP)服务器与Parsl工作流引擎交互。使用开源gpt-oss-120b模型协调CoRE MOF数据库的高通量筛选。

Result: 该智能体框架在Aurora超级计算机上实现了高效可扩展执行，具有低协调开销和高任务完成率。成功演示了用于大气水收集的金属有机框架数据库筛选。

Conclusion: 这项工作为HPC系统上的LLM驱动科学自动化建立了灵活范式，在材料发现等领域具有广泛适用性，解决了大规模LLM智能体部署的并行化挑战。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI) with High-Performance Computing (HPC) is transforming scientific workflows from human-directed pipelines into adaptive systems capable of autonomous decision-making. Large language models (LLMs) play a critical role in autonomous workflows; however, deploying LLM-based agents at scale remains a significant challenge. Single-agent architectures and sequential tool calls often become serialization bottlenecks when executing large-scale simulation campaigns, failing to utilize the massive parallelism of exascale resources. To address this, we present a scalable, hierarchical multi-agent framework for orchestrating high-throughput screening campaigns. Our planner-executor architecture employs a central planning agent to dynamically partition workloads and assign subtasks to a swarm of parallel executor agents. All executor agents interface with a shared Model Context Protocol (MCP) server that orchestrates tasks via the Parsl workflow engine. To demonstrate this framework, we employed the open-weight gpt-oss-120b model to orchestrate a high-throughput screening of the Computation-Ready Experimental (CoRE) Metal-Organic Framework (MOF) database for atmospheric water harvesting.
